This spacious and comfortable apartment offers a direct sea view and comes fully furnished. It features large balconies, a generous pantry together with a dining area, as well as a separate servant’s room and bathroom with a dedicated service entrance.

The apartment also includes access to a swimming pool, gymnasium, and all modern facilities and amenities, providing a convenient and luxurious living experience.

## Property Details

Property Details

Position your business for success with this versatile 1,400 sq. ft. office space for rent on kirulapana junction, Robert Gunawardena mawatha, Colombo 05. Located on the entire first floor and close to the main road, this property guarantees exceptional brand visibility, easy navigation, and high foot traffic.

The premises offers a highly adaptable, modern layout that can effortlessly accommodate a variety of corporate setups. Whether you are expanding an IT institution, launching a consultancy firm, setting up a ticketing agency, or establishing a regional back office, this space adapts to your exact operational needs.

Key Features & Layout

Spacious Adaptable Layout: The space features a massive open-plan section ideal for workstations or customer service areas, alongside two large private rooms perfect for executive cabins or meeting rooms.

Convenient Amenities: Includes one modern washroom, a dedicated pantry area for staff, and a prominent front balcony overlooking the private lane.

Flexible Access & Charm: Built with independent entrance for smooth traffic flow and accented by a beautifully maintained small garden area at the entrance.

Climate Control Ready: One room fully A/C and provisions are already in place to easily install air conditioning throughout the premises.

Ample Parking: Solves the Colombo parking hassle with 1 secure inside parking slot and an additional 2 to 3 dedicated guest parking slots directly in front of the premises.

Rental Terms & Pricing

Monthly Rent: LKR 195,000.00

Lease Commitment: Minimum 1 year

Service Fee: A standard half-month rental fee is applicable as an agent and service fee.

This is a rare opportunity to secure a highly functional, main-road commercial property in one of Colombo’s most affluent business districts.

Contact us today to arrange a private viewing and secure your new business address!

## Property Details

\*FOR RENT\*

A stunning 4 bedroom house for rent in a quiet lane on Fife Road Colombo 5. Ideal for those that want to be in a highly residential areas in one of the most accessible parts of Colombo. The house is spread over 3 floors and sits on a corner with access by 2 lanes. All bedrooms are air conditioned and have ensuite bathrooms. There is also a separate bathroom for the maid as well as another bathroom in the garage for the security guard! The property details are listed below by floor.

GROUND FLOOR

\- 2 car parking garage with Roller Gate

\- Garage Bathroom for Security Guard

\- Living and Dining Hall

\- 1 Bedroom with ensuite bathoom

\- Large Pantry as well as a separate wet kitchen

\- Maids Bathroom

FIRST FLOOR

\- 3 bedrooms with ensuite bathrooms

\- Living Area ideal to create a TV Lounge

ROOFTOP

\- Utility Room with washing machine outlets

\- 1 Bathroom

\- 1 Room that can be used as an extra bedroom or an Office/Storage

\- Rooftop Space

Ideal for Residential purpose. Ready for rent by December 1st 2025

## Property Details

Experience refined city living at 7th Sense Residencies, an exclusive residential address located on the prestigious Gregory’s Road in the heart of Colombo 7. This spacious 3 bedroom apartment spans approximately 2,100 sq.ft. and offers a perfect blend of comfort, privacy, and convenience. Situated on the 1st floor, the residence features 3 well appointed bedrooms, 3 bathrooms, bright and airy living and dining areas, a dedicated kitchen, separate maid’s quarters, and both a balcony and terrace with pleasant open views. Offered unfurnished, the apartment provides the flexibility to create a home tailored to your lifestyle. Ideally located within close proximity to leading international schools, embassies, supermarkets, cafes, and restaurants, this property is perfectly suited for families or professionals seeking upscale living in one of Colombo’s most sought after neighborhoods. Monthly rent: USD 3,600 (Negotiable).

## Property Details

Luxury Living Awaits at Havelock City – Available from August 2026

Experience the perfect blend of comfort, convenience, and modern city living in one of Colombo's most sought-after residential communities.

This beautifully maintained 3-bedroom fully furnished apartment offers an exceptional lifestyle within the prestigious Havelock City development. Featuring spacious interiors, tasteful furnishings, and a thoughtfully designed layout, this home is ideal for families, expatriates, and professionals seeking a premium residential experience in the heart of Colombo.

✨ Property Features:

• 3 Spacious Bedrooms

• Fully Furnished

• 1,335 Sqft of Comfortable Living Space

• Bright and Airy Living & Dining Area

• Modern Kitchen with Quality Appliances

• Well-Maintained Bathrooms

• Secure and Family-Friendly Environment

• Access to World-Class Havelock City Facilities

Residents can enjoy the convenience of living within a vibrant community offering 24-hour security, ample recreational facilities, landscaped surroundings, and easy access to shopping, dining, international schools, hospitals, and major business districts.

📍 Location: Havelock City, Colombo

💰 Rent: LKR 500,000 per Month

💵 Advance: 6 Months Upfront

🔐 Refundable Deposit: 2 Months Rent

📅 Available From: August 2026

A rare opportunity to secure a spacious, fully furnished 3-bedroom residence in one of Colombo's premier residential addresses.

## Property Details

Experience premium city living in this elegant 2 storey fully furnished penthouse located at the prestigious Green Path Residencies, Colombo 03. Designed for luxury, comfort, and privacy, this spacious residence features 3 well appointed bedrooms, 2 modern bathrooms, and a separate servant’s room with an attached bathroom, making it ideal for families, expatriates, or corporate professionals seeking an upscale lifestyle. Situated in the heart of Colombo, the property offers convenient access to the city's leading business districts, shopping destinations, restaurants, and entertainment venues. Available for a minimum one year lease, this exceptional penthouse is offered at a monthly rental of LKR 1,000,000, with a 6 month advance payment and a refundable 2 month security deposit required. A rare opportunity to enjoy sophisticated two level penthouse living in one of Colombo's most sought after residential developments.
